import copy,hashlib,importlib.util,json,pathlib,re,subprocess,sys
P=pathlib.Path(__file__).resolve().parent;R=pathlib.Path.cwd();C=P.parent/'candidate-install-integration'
def read(p):return json.loads(p.read_text(encoding='utf8'))
def save(n,v):(P/n).write_text(json.dumps(v,ensure_ascii=False,indent=2)+'\n',encoding='utf8')
def digest(p):return hashlib.sha256(p.read_bytes()).hexdigest()
spec=importlib.util.spec_from_file_location('candidate_subset',P/'schema_subset.py');m=importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec);spec.loader.exec_module(m)
roots={n:read(P/n) for n in ['request.schema.json','response.schema.json']};old={n:read(C/n) for n in roots}
f=read(P/'fixtures.json');nc=copy.deepcopy(f['response']);nc['value']={'outcome':'NoChange','projection':dict(f['response']['value']['projection'],revision='7'),'replayed':False,'revision':'7','history_depth':'2'}
deferred=copy.deepcopy(f['response']);deferred['value'].update(outcome='AppliedRetentionDeferred',stage='LiveRoots',reason='IoFailure')
bases={'request.schema.json':[f['request']],'response.schema.json':[f['response'],nc,deferred]+[{'ok':False,'error':{'code':code,'details':dict.fromkeys(keys,'7')},'replayed':False} for code,keys in [('StaleRevision',['expected','observed']),('SequenceConflict',['expected','received'])]]}
def accepts(s,x):
 try:m.Draft202012Validator(s).validate(x);return True
 except (AssertionError,ValueError,TypeError,KeyError):return False
def get(x,path):
 for k in path:x=x[k]
 return x
def mutate(base,path,value):
 x=copy.deepcopy(base);d=x
 for k in path[:-1]:d=d[k]
 d[path[-1]]=value;return x
trace=[]
def walk(root,s,pointer,path,refs,constraints):
 if '$ref'in s:
  ref=s['$ref'];target=roots[root]
  for k in ref[2:].split('/'):target=target[k]
  walk(root,target,ref[1:],path,refs+[{'at':pointer,'ref':ref}],constraints);return
 if s.get('pattern','').startswith('^(?:0|[1-9][0-9]'):
  valid=[]
  for b in bases[root]:
   try:get(b,path)
   except (KeyError,TypeError):continue
   if accepts(roots[root],b) and all(accepts(dict(z,**{'$defs':roots[root].get('$defs',{})}),get(b,at)) for z,at in constraints):valid.append(b)
  assert valid,(root,path)
  trace.append({'root':root,'path':path,'schema_pointer':pointer,'refs':refs,'base':valid[0]});return
 for k,v in s.get('properties',{}).items():walk(root,v,pointer+'/properties/'+k,path+[k],refs,constraints)
 for i,v in enumerate(s.get('oneOf',[])):walk(root,v,pointer+'/oneOf/'+str(i),path,refs,constraints+[(v,path)])
for root,s in roots.items():walk(root,s,'',[],[],[])
# Independent expected oracle uses lexical codepoints and integer boundary, never changes schema admission.
values=[('zero','0'),('one','1'),('max-1',str(2**64-2)),('max',str(2**64-1)),('max+1',str(2**64)),('LF','1\n'),('CR','1\r'),('CRLF','1\r\n'),('U2028','1\u2028'),('U2029','1\u2029'),('NUL','1\0'),('multiline','1\n2'),('prefix','x1'),('suffix','1x'),('leading-LF','\n1'),('empty',''),('leading-zero','01'),('plus','+1'),('space','1 '),('unicode-digit','١')]
def oracle(v):return bool(v) and all('0'<=c<='9' for c in v) and (v=='0' or v[0]!='0') and int(v)<=2**64-1
cases=[]
for i,t in enumerate(trace):
 for label,v in values:
  x=mutate(t['base'],t['path'],v);expected=oracle(v)
  cases.append({'id':f'CI-F1-{i:02}-{label}','root':t['root'],'path':t['path'],'occurrence':i,'label':label,'scalar':v,'codepoints':list(map(ord,v)),'expected':expected,'python':accepts(roots[t['root']],x),'python_before':accepts(old[t['root']],x),'instance':x})
js=r'''const fs=require('fs');const d=JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(0,'utf8'));
function valid(root,s,x){if(s.$ref){let t=root;for(const k of s.$ref.slice(2).split('/'))t=t[k];return valid(root,t,x)}
if(s.oneOf)return s.oneOf.filter(z=>valid(root,z,x)).length===1;
if('const'in s&&JSON.stringify(x)!==JSON.stringify(s.const))return false;
if(s.enum&&!s.enum.includes(x))return false;
const kind=x===null?'null':Array.isArray(x)?'array':typeof x;
if(s.type&&!(Array.isArray(s.type)?s.type:[s.type]).includes(kind))return false;
if(typeof x==='string'){if(x.length<(s.minLength||0)||x.length>(s.maxLength||1e9))return false;if(s.pattern&&!new RegExp(s.pattern).test(x))return false}
if(kind==='object'){if((s.required||[]).some(k=>!(k in x)))return false;if(s.additionalProperties===false&&Object.keys(x).some(k=>!(k in(s.properties||{}))))return false;if(Object.entries(s.properties||{}).some(([k,z])=>k in x&&!valid(root,z,x[k])))return false}
if(kind==='array'&&s.items&&x.some(z=>!valid(root,s.items,z)))return false;return true}
process.stdout.write(JSON.stringify(d.cases.map(c=>({id:c.id,accepted:valid(d.roots[c.root],d.roots[c.root],c.instance),before:valid(d.old[c.root],d.old[c.root],c.instance)}))));'''
(P/'ecmascript_root_checker.js').write_text(js,encoding='utf8')
node=json.loads(subprocess.check_output(['node',str(P/'ecmascript_root_checker.js')],input=json.dumps({'roots':roots,'old':old,'cases':cases}),text=True,encoding='utf8'))
for c,n in zip(cases,node):c['ecmascript']=n['accepted'];c['ecmascript_before']=n['before'];c['status']='PASS' if c['python']==c['ecmascript']==c['expected'] else 'FAIL';del c['instance']
assert all(c['status']=='PASS' for c in cases)
lf=[c for c in cases if c['label']=='LF'];assert all(c['python_before'] and not c['python'] and not c['ecmascript'] for c in lf)
authority=read(R/'docs/requirements-confirmation/phase1-spec/tasks/M01/planning/s2-green/source-current.json');drift=[p for p,h in authority['source_hashes'].items() if digest(R/p)!=h]
source=read(P/'correction-source-hashes.json');source_drift=[p for group in source.values() for p,h in group.items() if digest(R/p)!=h]
save('reachable-u64-trace.json',trace);save('new-probes.json',cases)
save('verification.json',{'status':'AUTHOR_SELF_CHECK_PASS_DOCUMENT_CANDIDATE_ONLY','author':'/root/m03_install_u64_correction','independent_acceptance':'NOT RUN; author cannot independently accept own packet','new_fullroot_cases':len(cases),'engine_executions':len(cases)*2,'passed':len(cases),'failed':0,'reachable_occurrences':len(trace),'original_LF_failures_closed':len(lf),'head':subprocess.check_output(['git','rev-parse','HEAD'],text=True).strip(),'branch':subprocess.check_output(['git','branch','--show-current'],text=True).strip(),'historical_authority_head':authority['head'],'authority_entries':len(authority['source_hashes']),'authority_drift':drift,'source_drift':source_drift,'tail_codepoints':list(map(ord,roots['request.schema.json']['$defs']['U64Decimal']['pattern'][-10:])),'preserved_historical_evidence':'104 author cases and 367 reviewer probes preserved by source hashes/references; not rerun or counted as new progress','not_run':['full Draft validator','product tests','native/UI/IPC','valid RED/GREEN','build/package/install','Owner adoption','independent acceptance']})
assert not drift and not source_drift
print(json.dumps({'new_fullroot_cases':len(cases),'occurrences':len(trace),'LF_closed':len(lf),'authority_entries':len(authority['source_hashes']),'failures':0}))
check_documents.py
"""Candidate DOCUMENT behavior model. Not product implementation or acceptance."""
import copy,json,pathlib,hashlib,uuid
from schema_subset import Draft202012Validator
P=pathlib.Path(__file__).parent
ROOT=pathlib.Path.cwd()
def read(n):return json.loads((P/n).read_text(encoding='utf8'))
def validate(req,res):
 Draft202012Validator(read('request.schema.json')).validate(req)
 Draft202012Validator(read('response.schema.json')).validate(res)
 if res['ok']:
  v=res['value'];assert v['replayed']==res['replayed']
  if v['outcome']=='NoChange':assert v['revision']==req['expected_revision']==v['projection']['revision']
  else:
   assert v['before_revision']==req['expected_revision']
   assert int(v['after_revision'])==int(v['before_revision'])+1
   assert v['projection']['revision']==v['after_revision']

def model(case):
 s=copy.deepcopy(case['state']);r=case['request']
 out={'outcome':None,'revision':s['live_revision'],'history':s['history_depth'],'redo':s['redo_depth'],'commits':0,'consume':False,'deliver':False,'render':False,'retain':True,'pin_state':s['pin_state'],'release':False,'FileCommit':False,'formal_ACK':False}
 def fail(code,uncertain=False):
  return dict(out,outcome=code,consume='Unknown' if uncertain else False)
 if not s['authorized']:return fail('UnauthorizedCaller')
 if s['closed']:return fail('MissingBinding')
 if not s['writer']:return fail('WriterRequired')
 if s['owner_generation']!=r['expected_generation']:return fail('StaleOwner')
 if s['terminal']:
  if s['capture']!=r:return fail('RequestConflict')
  return dict(out,**s['terminal'],deliver=True,render=s['live_revision']==s['terminal']['revision'],commits=0)
 if not s['candidate_present']:return fail('CandidateMissing')
 if s['candidate_used']:return fail('CandidateUsed')
 if s['candidate_generation']!=r['expected_generation']:return fail('StaleOwner')
 if s['live_revision']!=r['expected_revision'] or s['candidate_revision']!=r['expected_revision']:return fail('StaleRevision')
 if not all(s[k] for k in ['C1_ready','M04_ready','M05_ready']):return fail('DependencyNotReady')
 if s['owner_outcome']=='Unknown':return fail('UnverifiedOutcome',True)
 if not s['proof_verified'] or s['pin_state']!='Held':return fail('Indeterminate',True)
 if s['owner_outcome']=='NoChange':return dict(out,outcome='NoChange',consume=True,deliver=True,render=True)
 if int(s['live_revision'])==18446744073709551615:return fail('RevisionOverflow')
 return dict(out,outcome='AppliedRetentionDeferred' if s['root_stage'] else 'Applied',revision=str(int(s['live_revision'])+1),history=str(int(s['history_depth'])+1),redo='0',commits=1,consume=True,deliver=True,render=True)

def proofcheck(proof, trusted):
 # Lookup records are supplied immutable trusted premises; no caller body is an authority.
 assert proof['capture']==trusted['capture']
 for k in ['mutation','pin','roots']:assert proof[k]==trusted[k],k+' issuer record mismatch'
 c,m,p,z=(proof[k] for k in ['capture','mutation','pin','roots'])
 for record,role in [(m,'install'),(p,'pin'),(z,'transfer-live')]:
  assert record['ordinal']=='0' and record['operation_id']==str(uuid.uuid5(uuid.UUID(c['workflow_id']),role+'/0'))
 assert m['issuer']=='M04' and m['kind']=='DocumentMutation' and m['role']=='install'
 assert p['issuer']==z['issuer']=='M05' and p['purpose']=='Candidate'
 assert m['context']==p['context']==z['context']==c
 assert m['candidate_ref']==c['candidate_ref'] and m['pin_id']==p['pin_id']
 assert m['before_revision']==c['expected_revision'] and int(m['after_revision'])==int(m['before_revision'])+1
 assert int(m['history_after'])==int(m['history_before'])+1 and m['redo_after']=='0'
 assert p['actual_state']=='Held' and z['complete'] and z['fenced']
 kinds={'LiveDocuments','UndoHistory','RedoHistory','RecoverySnapshots','InFlightTransactions'}
 assert set(z['before'])==set(z['after'])==kinds
 pairs=lambda x:{tuple(v) for v in x}
 for kind in kinds:
  a,b=z['before'][kind],z['after'][kind]
  assert a['provider']==b['provider']=='fixture:'+kind
  assert a['epoch']==b['epoch']==z['epoch'] and a['complete'] and b['complete']
  assert int(b['version'])==int(a['version'])+(kind in {'LiveDocuments','UndoHistory','RedoHistory'})
 assert pairs(z['before']['LiveDocuments']['material_versions'])==pairs(m['outgoing'])
 assert pairs(z['after']['LiveDocuments']['material_versions'])==pairs(m['installed'])
 assert pairs(z['after']['UndoHistory']['material_versions'])==pairs(z['before']['UndoHistory']['material_versions'])|pairs(m['outgoing'])
 assert z['after']['RedoHistory']['material_versions']==[]
 # Only this fenced fixture has unchanged independent producers; no generic concurrency claim.
 for kind in ['RecoverySnapshots','InFlightTransactions']:assert z['before'][kind]==z['after'][kind]
 assert pairs(m['outgoing']+m['installed'])<=pairs(p['material_versions'])
 assert proof['io']=={'FileCommit':'NOT CALLED','formal_save_ack':'NOT CALLED','formal_root_transfer':'NOT CALLED'}

def main():
 results=[]
 def record(id,fn,expected=True):
  try:fn();actual=True
  except (AssertionError,ValueError,TypeError,KeyError):actual=False
  assert actual==expected,id
  results.append({'id':id,'expected_accept':expected,'observed_accept':actual,'status':'PASS_DOCUMENT_MODEL'})
 f=read('fixtures.json');req,res=f['request'],f['response']
 record('schema-positive',lambda:validate(req,res))
 nochange=copy.deepcopy(res);nochange['value']={'outcome':'NoChange','projection':dict(res['value']['projection'],revision='7'),'replayed':False,'revision':'7','history_depth':'2'}
 record('schema-NoChange',lambda:validate(req,nochange))
 bad=copy.deepcopy(req);bad['design_json']='{}'
 record('request-payload-injection',lambda:validate(bad,res),False)
 bad=copy.deepcopy(res);bad['value']['formal_ACK']=True
 record('response-formal-ACK-injection',lambda:validate(req,bad),False)
 for value in ['0','9007199254740993','18446744073709551615']:
  r=copy.deepcopy(req);r['expected_revision']=value
  record('u64-valid-'+value,lambda r=r:Draft202012Validator(read('request.schema.json')).validate(r))
 for value in ['18446744073709551616','99999999999999999999','01','-1','1.0',7,None,'']:
  r=copy.deepcopy(req);r['expected_revision']=value
  record('u64-reject-'+str(value),lambda r=r:Draft202012Validator(read('request.schema.json')).validate(r),False)
 for value in ['18446744073709551616',7,'01']:
  r=copy.deepcopy(res);r['value']['projection']['owner_generation']=value
  record('response-u64-'+str(value),lambda r=r:validate(req,r),False)
 for code in ['NoUndo','RevisionOverflow','Closed','RevisionConflict','NotReady','Busy','InvalidInput']:
  fields={'field':'expected_revision'} if code=='InvalidInput' else {}
  for replay in [False,True]:
   r={'ok':False,'error':{'code':'DomainRejected','details':{'code':code,'fields':fields}},'replayed':replay}
   record('domain-'+code+'-'+str(replay),lambda r=r:validate(req,r))
   for bad in ['',dict(fields,unexpected='x')]:
    b=copy.deepcopy(r);b['error']['details']['fields']=bad
    record('domain-reject-'+code+'-'+str(replay)+'-'+str(bad),lambda b=b:validate(req,b),False)
 for c in f['lifecycle']:
  actual=model(c)
  def check(c=c,actual=actual):
   for k,v in c['expected'].items():assert actual[k]==v,(c['id'],k,actual[k],v)
   assert actual['release'] is False and actual['FileCommit'] is False and actual['formal_ACK'] is False
  record(c['id'],check)
 trusted=read('trusted-fixture-authority.json');record('proof-complete',lambda:proofcheck(trusted,trusted))
 mutations=[('mutation','issuer','M05'),('mutation','after_revision','9'),('mutation','candidate_ref','other'),('pin','actual_state','Unknown'),('pin','material_versions',[]),('roots','fenced',False),('roots','complete',False)]
 for section,key,v in mutations:
  p=copy.deepcopy(trusted);p[section][key]=v
  record('proof-tamper-'+section+'-'+key,lambda p=p:proofcheck(p,trusted),False)
 # Relation checks also exercised independently from lookup equality by deliberately
 # constructing alternate *fixture premises*. These are relation tests, not attacks.
 for kind,key,v in [('LiveDocuments','material_versions',[['X','v1']]),('UndoHistory','material_versions',[]),('RedoHistory','material_versions',[['Z','v1']]),('LiveDocuments','version','9'),('RecoverySnapshots','version','3')]:
  p=copy.deepcopy(trusted);p['roots']['after'][kind][key]=v
  record('relation-'+kind+'-'+key,lambda p=p:proofcheck(p,p),False)
 p=copy.deepcopy(trusted);p['pin']['material_versions']=[]
 record('relation-pin-membership',lambda:proofcheck(p,p),False)
 for path,h in read('source-hashes.json').items():assert hashlib.sha256((ROOT/path).read_bytes()).hexdigest()==h,path
 verification={'status':'AUTHOR_DOCUMENT_MODEL_CHECK_ONLY','author':'/root/m03_candidate_install_packet','independent_acceptance':'PENDING_SEPARATE_REVIEW','cases':len(results),'results':results,'fullDraft':'NOT RUN; jsonschema dependency MISSING; generic subset interpreter is not full Draft conformance','product_tests':'NOT RUN','native':'NOT RUN','RED':'NOT RUN','GREEN':'NOT RUN','build':'NOT RUN','Owner_adoption':'NOT ADOPTED','module_complete':False}
 (P/'verification.json').write_text(json.dumps(verification,ensure_ascii=False,indent=2)+'\n',encoding='utf8')
 print(json.dumps({'document_cases':len(results),'status':verification['status']}))
if __name__=='__main__':main()

correction.feature
Feature: Strict bounded decimal admission in the candidate install document
  Background:
    Given an otherwise valid candidate install request or response
  Rule: Numeric decimal fields admit only canonical unsigned 64-bit strings
    Scenario: Reject an actual terminal line feed
      When a numeric decimal contains "1" followed by an actual line feed
      Then the complete document is rejected
    Scenario: Admit the upper bound
      When a numeric decimal is "18446744073709551615"
      Then its numeric schema admits the complete document
    Scenario: Reject overflow
      When a numeric decimal is "18446744073709551616"
      Then the complete document is rejected
ecmascript_root_checker.js
const fs=require('fs');const d=JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(0,'utf8'));
function valid(root,s,x){if(s.$ref){let t=root;for(const k of s.$ref.slice(2).split('/'))t=t[k];return valid(root,t,x)}
if(s.oneOf)return s.oneOf.filter(z=>valid(root,z,x)).length===1;
if('const'in s&&JSON.stringify(x)!==JSON.stringify(s.const))return false;
if(s.enum&&!s.enum.includes(x))return false;
const kind=x===null?'null':Array.isArray(x)?'array':typeof x;
if(s.type&&!(Array.isArray(s.type)?s.type:[s.type]).includes(kind))return false;
if(typeof x==='string'){if(x.length<(s.minLength||0)||x.length>(s.maxLength||1e9))return false;if(s.pattern&&!new RegExp(s.pattern).test(x))return false}
if(kind==='object'){if((s.required||[]).some(k=>!(k in x)))return false;if(s.additionalProperties===false&&Object.keys(x).some(k=>!(k in(s.properties||{}))))return false;if(Object.entries(s.properties||{}).some(([k,z])=>k in x&&!valid(root,z,x[k])))return false}
if(kind==='array'&&s.items&&x.some(z=>!valid(root,s.items,z)))return false;return true}
process.stdout.write(JSON.stringify(d.cases.map(c=>({id:c.id,accepted:valid(d.roots[c.root],d.roots[c.root],c.instance),before:valid(d.old[c.root],d.old[c.root],c.instance)}))));

  Background:
    Given an otherwise valid candidate install request or response
  Rule: Numeric decimal fields admit only canonical unsigned 64-bit strings
    Scenario: Reject an actual terminal line feed
      When a numeric decimal contains "1" followed by an actual line feed
      Then the complete document is rejected
    Scenario: Admit the upper bound
      When a numeric decimal is "18446744073709551615"

      Then the complete document is rejected

(P/'lessons.md').write_text('''# CI-IR-F1 root cause and prevention

The command packet rebuilt a bounded U64 regex with `$`. Python `re.search` lets `$` match before a terminal LF. Earlier vector success did not prove strict end-of-input behavior. The independent review reproduced 13 whole-root LF admissions; ECMAScript behavior differed.

Reuse the already reviewed `save-proof-correction/request.schema.json` `$defs/U64Decimal` object, including minLength/maxLength and the parsed single-backslash `(?![\\s\\S])` strict end assertion. lessons.md
# CI-IR-F1 root cause and prevention

The command packet rebuilt a bounded U64 regex with `$`. Python `re.search` lets `$` match before a terminal LF. Earlier vector success did not prove strict end-of-input behavior. The independent review reproduced 13 whole-root LF admissions; ECMAScript behavior differed.

Reuse the already reviewed `save-proof-correction/request.schema.json` `$defs/U64Decimal` object, including minLength/maxLength and the parsed single-backslash `(?![\s\S])` strict end assertion. Do not recreate a command-specific U64 grammar. Future command packets must reuse this reviewed definition and run actual control-codepoint, max-bound and fullroot branch/reference probes. No field-name custom hook or fullmatch bypass may mask a schema error.

The correction inventories four definition sites, including inline request fields and both `$defs`. Reachability expands to 20 branch/reference occurrences. Branch-specific valid base fixtures distinguish Applied, NoChange and AppliedRetentionDeferred, plus both error arms. This includes every original 13 reviewer path/branch case. New 400 cases run in two engines; old 104/367 evidence is preserved, not recounted as progress.

This is author self-check only. Independent acceptance, full Draft validation, native product behavior and valid TDD RED remain NOT RUN. Candidate grammar correction does not adopt architecture or change NoChange/consume/retention/replay policy.

module.feature
Feature: Install a prepared design candidate safely
  Background:
    Given one authoritative document owner and a trusted current writer
    And a supported candidate prepared through the validated codec
  Rule: Candidate installation respects owner authority and retained outcomes

prepare_candidate.py
import ast,copy,hashlib,html,json,pathlib,re,shutil,subprocess
P=pathlib.Path(__file__).resolve().parent; R=pathlib.Path.cwd(); C=P.parent/'candidate-install-integration'; S=P.parent/'save-proof-correction'
def read(p):return json.loads(p.read_text(encoding='utf8'))
def save(n,v):(P/n).write_text(json.dumps(v,ensure_ascii=False,indent=2)+'\n',encoding='utf8')
def digest(p):return hashlib.sha256(p.read_bytes()).hexdigest()
# Copy the complete command candidate. Original checker/104 trace retained, not rerun.
for p in C.iterdir():
 if p.is_file():shutil.copyfile(p,P/p.name)
shutil.copyfile(C/'verification.json',P/'historical-author-verification.json')
shutil.copyfile(C/'index.html',P/'historical-candidate.html')
strict=read(S/'request.schema.json')['$defs']['U64Decimal']
assert list(map(ord,strict['pattern'][-10:]))==[40,63,33,91,92,115,92,83,93,41]
changes=[]
def patch(s,path):
 if isinstance(s,dict):
  if 'pattern'in s and s['pattern'].startswith('^(0|[1-9][0-9]'):
   changes.append({'pointer':path,'before':copy.deepcopy(s),'after':copy.deepcopy(strict)})
   s.clear();s.update(copy.deepcopy(strict));return
  for k,v in s.items():patch(v,path+'/'+k.replace('~','~0').replace('/','~1'))
 elif isinstance(s,list):
  for i,v in enumerate(s):patch(v,path+'/'+str(i))
for name in ['request.schema.json','response.schema.json']:
 before=read(C/name);after=copy.deepcopy(before);start=len(changes);patch(after,'');save(name,after)
 for c in changes[start:]:c['file']=name
save('schema-pointer-diff.json',changes)
# A normalization proof: replacing each exact changed node with its original recreates source JSON exactly.
equiv=[]
for name in ['request.schema.json','response.schema.json']:
 restored=read(P/name)
 for c in changes:
  if c['file']!=name:continue
  keys=c['pointer'][1:].split('/');node=restored
  for k in keys[:-1]:node=node[int(k)] if isinstance(node,list) else node[k.replace('~1','/').replace('~0','~')]
  node[keys[-1]]=c['before']
 canonical=lambda x:json.dumps(x,sort_keys=True,separators=(',',':')).encode()
 a=canonical(read(C/name));b=canonical(restored);assert a==b
 equiv.append({'file':name,'non_U64_canonical_equivalent':True,'original_sha256':digest(C/name),'candidate_sha256':digest(P/name),'restored_canonical_sha256':hashlib.sha256(b).hexdigest()})
unchanged=[{'file':p.name,'before_sha256':digest(p),'after_sha256':digest(P/p.name),'bytes_equal':p.read_bytes()==(P/p.name).read_bytes()} for p in C.iterdir() if p.is_file() and p.name not in ['request.schema.json','response.schema.json','index.html','verification.json','artifact-hashes.json']]
assert all(x['bytes_equal'] for x in unchanged)
save('non-u64-equivalence.json',{'schemas':equiv,'unchanged':unchanged,'policies':'NoChange/consume/retention/replay/proof/model unchanged byte-for-byte; no new policy adoption'})
save('correction-source-hashes.json',{'candidate_source':{str(p.relative_to(R)).replace('\\','/'):digest(p) for p in C.iterdir() if p.is_file()},'strict_definition':{str((S/'request.schema.json').relative_to(R)).replace('\\','/'):digest(S/'request.schema.json')},'review':{str((P.parent/'candidate-install-review/review.json').relative_to(R)).replace('\\','/'):digest(P.parent/'candidate-install-review/review.json')}})
print('complete copy; schema occurrences',len(changes))

schema_subset.py
import re
import copy
class Draft202012Validator:
 def __init__(self,s):self.s=s
 @staticmethod
 def check_schema(s):assert s.get('$schema')=='https://json-schema.org/draft/2020-12/schema'
 def validate(self,x):
  def v(s,x):
   if '$ref' in s:
    t=self.s
    for k in s['$ref'][2:].split('/'):t=t[k]
    return v(t,x)
   if 'oneOf' in s:return sum(v(z,x) for z in s['oneOf'])==1
   if 'const' in s and (type(x)!=type(s['const']) or x!=s['const']):return False
   if 'enum' in s and x not in s['enum']:return False
   kinds={'object':type(x)is dict,'array':type(x)is list,'string':type(x)is str,'boolean':type(x)is bool,'null':x is None}
   t=s.get('type')
   if t and not any(kinds.get(k,False) for k in (t if isinstance(t,list) else [t])):return False
   if type(x)is str:
    if not s.get('minLength',0)<=len(x)<=s.get('maxLength',10**9):return False
    if 'pattern'in s and re.search(s['pattern'],x)is None:return False
   if type(x)is dict:
    if any(k not in x for k in s.get('required',[])):return False
    if s.get('additionalProperties')is False and any(k not in s.get('properties',{}) for k in x):return False
    if any(k in x and not v(z,x[k]) for k,z in s.get('properties',{}).items()):return False
   if type(x)is list and 'items'in s and any(not v(s['items'],z) for z in x):return False
   return True
  assert v(self.s,x),'schema rejection'
